===== [p120-120] INTRODUCTION > Introduction (2块) =====
--[p120]-- Introduction
Offline Feeder Setup program is designed to correctly place feeders and parts on the trolley within as short time as specified in PRG job program and to minimize the feeder replacement time by performing the feeding test offline.
It compares PRG job program prepared by (Offline) Program Editor and the parameters in the tray feeder, adjusts the parameters, and stores the adjusted ones in the job program and the feeder.
--[p120]-- Plus, it prevents wrong components from being inserted, and updates parameters and barcode labels if required, using the barcode system.

===== [p32-32] Main Features productivity (2块) =====
--[p32]-- If you press the TROLLEY LOCK button after docking the equipment, the feeder base is adjusted and the interface board is automatically connected with power and communication lines. The equipment will return to the trolley docking status if you press the TROLLEY UNLUCK button.
--[p32]-- Main Features productivity
1. Ensures remarkably reduced job change over time and higher
The equipment reduces the job change over time from 3 hours to 30 seconds or less. If job change must be performed five times (max) a day to meet the requirements of small production of various models, job change time is reduced from 15 hours to 3 minutes or less.
2. One touch button maximizes user convenience

===== [p8-9] Procedure (2块) =====
--[p8]-- Procedure
Reel mounting
1. Place the feeder in a safe place (reel hanger).
2. Leave the reel and vinyl about 200mm respectively from the vinyl off point. (Refer to the figure in Page 7.)
3. Mount the reel on the Reel Hanger'.
4. Adjust the reel to the Reel Path'. (Refer to the figure in Page 8.)
5. Release the reel cover locking lever' and open the Reel Cover'.
6. Move the reel and set the vinyl off point to the 'Vinyl Off Slot' of the reel cover.
7.
--[p9]-- Set the vinyl to go through the 'Vinyl Off Slot'.
8. Adjust the reel to be engaged in Feeding Gear'.
9. Adjust the ‘Shutter' position, and close the Reel Cover'. (Refer to the figure in Page 9.)
10. Set the vinyl from the 'Vinyl Off Slot' to be engaged in the 'Vinyl Off Gear'.
11. Wind the 'Vinyl Off Gear' so that the vinyl gets tight. (Refer to the figure in Page 10.)
12. Use the Manual Feeding Lever' to have the chip placed before Pickup Position'.
More than 200\~250mm

===== [p94-100] Setup > \* Measurement (7块) =====
--[p100]-- Checking surface treatment on exposed parts
Check the machine parts vulnerable to corrosion as follows:
1.Power off the machine.
2.Check for rust on the parts directly exposed to the air.
3.If rust is found, remove it with a rust remover and wipe the surface using a dry cloth.
4.If parts without painting, coating or anti-corrosive coating are stained with foreign materials or water, wipe the surface with a dry cloth.
--[p97]-- (Wipe the opposite LM Guide in the same way.)
7.Wipe the grease flowing out from the LM Guide Block.
Regularly check the timing belt to ensure the Y-axis assembly operates normally.
1.Power off the machine according to the normal power off procedure.
2.Measure the tension of the Y belt using a belt tension meter.
3.If the tension exceeds the rated tension (40±5N), slightly loosen the tension setting pulley with a wrench and set to a proper tension using the setting bolt of the Tension
--[p95]-- 3.Wipe the grease and dust from the Ball Screw with a clean cloth. (Wipe the opposite Ball Screw in the same way.)
4.Manually move the Ball Screw Block toward the opposite edge.
5.Wipe the grease and dust from the Ball Screw with a clean cloth. (Wipe the opposite Ball Screw in the same way.)
6.Fill grease through the grease inlet of the Ball Screw Nut. (Wipe the opposite Bass Screw in the same way.)
7.Wipe the grease flowing out of the Ball Screw Nut.
--[p96]-- Maintenance of the Y-axis LM Guide should be done as follows:
1.Turn off the power of the machine.
2.Manually move the Carrier Arm to the pallet loading position of the magazine.
3.Wipe the grease and dust from the LM Guide with a clean cloth.
4.Manually move the Carrier Arm to the end of Mounter Picking position.
5.Wipe the grease and dust from the LM Guide with a clean cloth.
6.Fill grease through the grease inlet of the LM Guide Block.
7.Wipe the grease flowing out of the LM Guide Block.
--[p94]-- \* Measurement
-Place a pallet on a flat surface with Side A toward the floor.
-A pallet with a gap of at least 1mm between Side A and the floor is no good.
-A pallet with a gap of at least 1mm between Side C and the floor is no good.
-A pallet with a gap of at least 0.5mm between Side B and the floor is no good.
\* See the following figures for measurement.
Maintenance of Z-axis ball screw should be done as follows.
1.Power off the machine.
2.Manually move the Ball Screw Block toward the edge.
--[p97]-- Maintenance of the Z-axis LM Guide should be done as follows:
1.Turn off the power of the machine.
2.Manually move the LM Guide to the edge.
3.Wipe the grease and dust from the LM Guide with a clean cloth. (Wipe the opposite LM Guide in the same way.)
4.Manually move the LM Guide to the opposite edge.
5.Wipe the grease and dust from the LM Guide with a clean cloth. (Wipe the opposite LM Guide in the same way.)
6.Fill grease through the grease inlet of the two LM Guide Blocks.
--[p99]-- Setting Block.
4.Firmly fix the tension setting pulley.
Regularly check the timing belt to ensure the Z-axis assembly operates normally.
1.Power off the machine according to the normal power off process.
2.Measure the tension of the Z belt using a belt tension meter.
3.If the tension exceeds the rated tension (50±5N), slightly loosen the Tension Z Motor Assembly with a wrench and set to a proper tension with the setting bolt of the tension setting block.
4.Firmly fix the Z Motor Assembly.

To prevent mechanical damage, a mechanical part should be replaced before it causes mechanical problems, considering the lifetime of the part. A machine stained with dust or foreign materials will not provide optimum performance.

===== [p33-33] 3. Improved safety functions (1块) =====
--[p33]-- 3. Improved safety functions
As the trolley is locked via Trolley Up, there is no possibility of collision with head or nozzle by malfunction. The trolley is designed not to move during the operation of the equipment. The feeder base does not fall down even for abnormal disconnection of air pressure.

===== [p144-144] SOFTWARE AND HARDWARE INSTALLATION > Feeder Data Display Table (4块) =====
--[p144]-- Feeder Data Display Table
Feeder Data Display Table is used for displaying the feeder data read from the feeder when you click Read Feeder button and the data read from the PRG you opened by clicking the Open button. It is also used for displaying the processing status of the data read from the barcode reader.
This table shows Hole No, S/W ID, Part Number, Remain #, Alarm #, Pitch, Width and Feeding # as shown in the following figure.
--[p144]-- 1 | 0 | 1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
10 | 0 | 1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
11 | 0 | 1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
23 | 0 | 1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
51 | 0 | 1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
52 | 0 | R1005 | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 8mm | 0
74 | 0 | 118(102105) | 0 | 0 | 2mm | 24mm | 0
75 | 0 | 100(102403) | 0 | 0 | 4mm | 12mm | 0
101 | 0 | 10959 | 0 | 0 | 4mm | Default | 0
104 | 0 | 10959 | 0 | 0 | 4mm | Default | 0
201 | 0 | 10959 | 0 | 0 | Amm | Dofeult | 0
--[p144]-- Here, the lines are marked in gray as shown in the following figure in case that: the feeders are set as not to be used for PRG, they are tray feeders, or the feeders not specified in PRG.
You have to scan the parts or a feeder number barcode while a cell is selected in this table. When the feeder number barcode value is scanned, it moves to the row corresponding to the extracted feeder number.
--[p144 [表]]-- When the scanned part barcode is different from the part name in the row selected in the table, an error message is displayed and when they are matched, Remain field is updated. To save the updated Remain data to the feeder, you have to click Write Feeder button and to save it to PRG, you have to click Save button.
<Fig. 4-7、Feeder Data Display Table> Data in each column of the table has the following meaning:
Hole No | SAWID | Part Number | Remain # | Alarm # | Pitch | Width | Feeding #

===== [p153-153] SOFTWARE AND HARDWARE INSTALLATION > Safety Instructions (4块) =====
--[p153]-- Unplug the machine when replacing electric parts, sensors or solenoid valves.
9. Unplug the machine as removing connectors.
10.Follow recommended operating conditions (ambient temperature, humidity, power, pneumatic pressure, etc.)
11. Keep away from flammable gases, contaminants or excessive dust.
12. Do not put hands, head or other body parts into the system during operation.
13. Do not use the PC connected to the system for applications other than running the system.
--[p153]-- Safety Instructions
The following guidelines should be observed to avoid safety accidents and malfunctions that could be caused by incorrect operation of the machine.
1. For safety purpose, read this manual carefully and observe the safety labels on the system. For further information, see the maintenance manual.
2. Find out where Emergency Switch' is; activate the switch in case of emergency.
3. Do not work with wet hands. Electric shock could occur
4.
--[p153]-- The computer could be infected by external viruses.
14.The system should be operated only by personnel who completed the regular system operation training program.
15.The system should be maintained only by personnel who completed Mirae Corporation's maintenance program or an equivalent program.
16. Contact service personnel regarding other topics not described in the manual.
17. Make sure to observe the manual when working with the machine.
--[p153]-- Open the cover after unplugging unit from power source.
5. Do not operate the system after shifting the door safety sensor manually, Manual control of the safety sensor can cause malfunction.
6. Check all safety instructions when reactivate the system. Pay extra attention when two or more persons operating the machine at once.
7. Do not allow water or oil to get into electrical parts (VME rack, Power Box, Amp, PC, boards, etc.)
8.

===== [p79-79] Setup > -. Pallet State (1块) =====
--[p79]-- -. Pallet State
The left field shows the current pallet position.
When the Push button is pressed, the pallet moves from the Mounter to the rack. At this time, the left field displays “In Rack”.
When the Pull button is pressed, the pallet moves from rack to the Mounter. At this time, the left field displays “Not In Rack”.

--[p129]-- If the printer driver has not been correctly set up, such information is not displayed. In this case, you shall reinstall the driver.

If the information displayed on the "Barcode Print" window is printed as it is, it means that the printer driver has been correctly set up.

===== [p87-87] Setup > Z Axis (1块) =====
--[p87]-- Z Axis
▶Side Frame Assembly: The base frame of the tray feeder acting as the base of X-axis assembly as well as the main frame of the Tray Feeder.
▶Magazine Assembly: A Z-axis part that moves up and down along the Z-axis to load the pallet on the Y axis.
▶Pallet Assembly: A plate that loads tray containing various parts.   
Protects tray and helps safe loading of tray.

Program Functions
Offline Feeder Setup program is designed to correctly place feeders and parts on the trolley within as short time as specified in PRG job program and to minimize the feeder replacement time by performing the feeding test offline.
Program functions are as follows:
--[p122]-- ④ Read Intelligent Feeder (IF) Data (All or Each Feeder) (IF Data: SWID, Part Number, Remain#, Alarm#, Pitch, Width, Feeding#)
4 Edit IF Data
4 Save IF Data (All or Each Feeder)
④ IF Feeding Test (All or Selected Feeder, Repeat Mode or not, set Count and Delay)
④ Load, Save, and Close PRG File
4 Comparison PRG and IF Data
4 Display the Comparison Result
4 Read Part Bar Code and update data (Barcode String, Remain Count)
④ Read Feeder No. Bar Code and automatically change current feeder
4 Print Part Bar Code
④ Print Feeder No. Bar Code

displays the result as shown in the following figure.
--[p146]-- In this Status Display window, you can update the feeder data by reading PRG or clicking Read Feeder button.
Feeders placed without PRG : Gray  Feeders set as not to be used in PRG: Gray  Feeders with no feeders placed : Yellow  Feeders with different part numbers: Aqua  Feeders with different widths : Purple  Feeders with different pitches : Olive Feeders with different remain counts : Fuchsia PRG data and feeder data are the same: Blue, Navy

===== [p33-33] 4. Reasonable trolley price (1块) =====
--[p33]-- 4. Reasonable trolley price
As the trolley locking/unlocking actuator (cylinder) is imbedded inside the equipment, the trolley consists of simple parts such as height controller, guiding part and feeder base. Therefore, the price and weight of the trolley become more reasonable than those of other vendor equipments.

===== [p137-137] SOFTWARE AND HARDWARE INSTALLATION > Open (1块) =====

===== [p173-173] Guidelines to using this Manual > Discarding Assembly (1块) =====
--[p173]-- Discarding Assembly
▶Check for defective parts such as vision lead damage and decide whether to place the part picked up by the nozzle. If no part is picked up due to picking error, or a part is picked up vertically due to pickup position error or parts feeding error, the part is placed in the empty tray on the tray plate.
\* When placing the part in the empty tray put it in the same direction as parts feeding.
